      <header className="topbar">
        <div className="brand-mark">Ã¢â„¢Å¾</div>
        <button className="icon-button library-toggle" aria-label="Toggle library" onClick={() => setLibraryOpen((value) => !value)}>Ã¢ËœÂ°</button>
        <div className="brand-name">ChessBook <span>Reader</span></div>
        <div className="top-divider" />
        <div className="current-book" title={book?.title ?? "No book open"}>
          <span className="book-dot" />
          <strong>{book?.title ?? "No book open"}</strong>
          {book && <span className="muted">{session?.page ?? 1} / {session?.pageCount || "Ã¢â‚¬â€"}</span>}
        </div>
        <div className="top-spacer" />
        <span className="storage-label"><i /> LOCAL LIBRARY</span>
        <button className="icon-button" aria-label="Settings" onClick={() => setSettingsOpen((value) => !value)}>Ã¢Å¡â„¢</button>
      </header>

      <div className={`workspace ${libraryOpen ? "" : "library-collapsed"}`}>
        {libraryOpen && (
          <aside className="library-panel">
            <div className="library-heading"><span>LIBRARY</span><button className="text-button" onClick={() => fileInput.current?.click()}>Ã¯Â¼â€¹ Add Book</button></div>
            <input
              ref={fileInput}
              className="hidden-input"
              type="file"
              accept="application/pdf,.pdf"
              onChange={(event) => void addBook(event.target.files?.[0])}
            />
            <label className="search-field"><span>Ã¢Å’â€¢</span><input value={bookSearch} onChange={(event) => setBookSearch(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search library" /></label>
            <nav className="library-nav" aria-label="Library sections">
              <LibraryNavItem active={libraryTab === "all"} onClick={() => setLibraryTab("all")} icon="Ã¢â€“Â¤" label="All Books" />
              <LibraryNavItem active={libraryTab === "recent"} onClick={() => setLibraryTab("recent")} icon="Ã¢â€”Â·" label="Recently Opened" />
              <LibraryNavItem active={libraryTab === "bookmarks"} onClick={() => setLibraryTab("bookmarks")} icon="Ã¢Å¡â€˜" label="Bookmarks" />
              <LibraryNavItem active={libraryTab === "positions"} onClick={() => setLibraryTab("positions")} icon="Ã¢â„¢Å¸" label="Saved Positions" />
              <LibraryNavItem active={libraryTab === "progress"} onClick={() => setLibraryTab("progress")} icon="Ã¢â€”Â´" label="Reading Progress" />
              <LibraryNavItem active={libraryTab === "local"} onClick={() => setLibraryTab("local")} icon="Ã¢â€“Â£" label="Local Books" />
            </nav>
            <div className="library-list-title"><span>{libraryTab === "recent" ? "RECENTLY OPENED" : libraryTab.toUpperCase()}</span><span>{visibleBooks.length}</span></div>
            <div className="book-list">
              {visibleBooks.map((item, index) => {
                const saved = sessionIndex[item.id];
                const progress = saved?.pageCount ? Math.min(100, Math.round((saved.page / saved.pageCount) * 100)) : 0;
                return (
                  <div key={item.id} className={`book-row ${book?.id === item.id ? "active" : ""}`}>
                    <button className="book-open" onClick={() => void openBook(item)} aria-label={`Open ${item.title}`}>
                      <div className="book-cover"><span>Ã¢â„¢Å¸</span><small>{index + 1}</small></div>
                      <span className="book-meta"><strong>{item.title}</strong><small>Page {saved?.page ?? 1}{saved?.pageCount ? ` of ${saved.pageCount}` : ""} Ã‚Â· {readableDate(item.lastOpenedAt)}</small><span className="progress-track"><i style={{ width: `${progress}%` }} /></span></span>
                    </button>
                    <details className="book-menu"><summary aria-label={`Options for ${item.title}`}>Ã‚Â·Ã‚Â·Ã‚Â·</summary><div className="book-menu-items"><button onClick={() => void renameBook(item)}>Rename</button><button onClick={() => void deleteBook(item)}>Remove local copy</button></div></details>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
              {!visibleBooks.length && <div className="empty-library"><div>Ã¢â€“Â¤</div><strong>{books.length ? "Nothing in this view" : "Your shelf is empty"}</strong><span>Add a PDF to begin reading. Your books stay in this browser.</span><button className="green-button" onClick={() => fileInput.current?.click()}>Ã¯Â¼â€¹ Add Book</button></div>}
            </div>
            <div className="library-footer"><span className="local-dot" /> Stored on this device <span className="footer-size">{formatSize(books.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.byteLength, 0))}</span></div>
          </aside>
        )}

        <section className="board-panel" aria-label="Chessboard analysis">
          <div className="section-kicker"><span>POSITION ANALYSIS</span><span className={`engine-idle engine-${engineStatus}`}><i /> {engineStatus === "off" ? "ENGINE OFF" : `ENGINE ${engineStatus.toUpperCase()}`}</span></div>
          <div className="board-header">
            <div><strong>{session?.selectedDiagramId ? "Selected diagram" : "Analysis board"}</strong><span>{session?.selectedDiagramId ? `Page ${session.page} Ã‚Â· ${session.selectedDiagramId.startsWith("manual-") ? "Manual region" : "Board candidate"}` : "Ready for a position"}</span></div>
            <button className="mini-button" onClick={() => patchSession({ flipped: !session?.flipped })} title="Flip board">Ã¢â€ Â» Flip</button>
          </div>
          <div className="board-area">
            <div
              className={`evaluation-bar ${engineScore ? "evaluation-ready" : ""}`}
              aria-label={engineScore ? `Evaluation ${formatEvaluation(engineScore)} from White's perspective` : engineStatus === "error" ? "Engine evaluation unavailable" : "Waiting for engine evaluation"}
              title={engineScore ? `White's perspective Ã‚Â· depth ${engineScore.depth}` : engineStatus === "error" ? engineMessage : "No valid engine score yet"}
            >
              <div className="eval-white" style={{ height: `${engineScore ? whiteScoreShare(engineScore) * 100 : 50}%` }} />
              <div className="eval-black" style={{ height: `${engineScore ? (1 - whiteScoreShare(engineScore)) * 100 : 50}%` }} />
            </div>
            <div className="board-frame">
              <div
                className={`chessboard ${session?.flipped ? "flipped" : ""} ${annotationMode ? "annotation-mode" : ""}`}
                ref={boardRef}
                onPointerDown={boardArrowPointerDown}
                onPointerMove={boardArrowPointerMove}
                onPointerUp={boardArrowPointerUp}
                onPointerCancel={cancelBoardArrowGesture}
                onContextMenu={(event) => {
                  if (suppressBoardContextMenuRef.current) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    suppressBoardContextMenuRef.current = false;
                  }
                }}
              >
                {Array.from({ length: 64 }, (_, index) => {
                  const visibleRow = Math.floor(index / 8);
                  const visibleCol = index % 8;
                  const fileIndex = session?.flipped ? 7 - visibleCol : visibleCol;
                  const rankIndex = session?.flipped ? visibleRow : 7 - visibleRow;
                  const square = squareName(fileIndex, rankIndex);
                  const piece = pieceSquares[7 - rankIndex]?.[fileIndex];
                  const legal = legalTargets.has(square);
                  const isLast = highlightedMove?.from === square || highlightedMove?.to === square;
                  const light = (fileIndex + rankIndex) % 2 === 1;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={square}
                      className={`square ${light ? "light-square" : "dark-square"} ${selectedSquare === square ? "selected-square" : ""} ${isLast ? "last-square" : ""}`}
                      aria-label={`${square}${piece ? ` ${piece.color === "w" ? "white" : "black"} ${piece.type}` : ""}`}
                      draggable={Boolean(piece && piece.color === game.turn())}
                      onClick={() => handleSquareClick(square)}
                      onDragStart={(event) => {
                        const image = event.currentTarget.querySelector("svg");
                        if (image) {
                          const bounds = event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
                          event.dataTransfer.setDragImage(image, bounds.width / 2, bounds.height / 2);
                        }
                        event.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain", square);
                        setSelectedSquare(square);
                      }}
                      onDragOver={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
                      onDrop={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); const from = event.dataTransfer.getData("text/plain") as Square; if (from) makeMove(from, square); }}
                      onDragEnd={() => setSelectedSquare(null)}
                    >
                      {piece && <span className={`piece ${piece.color === "w" ? "white-piece" : "black-piece"}`} aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 40 40" focusable="false"><use href={`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}assets/pieces/standard.svg#${piece.color}${piece.type}`} /></svg></span>}
                      {legal && <span className={piece ? "legal-capture" : "legal-dot"} />}
                      {session?.coordinates && visibleCol === 0 && <span className={`rank-coordinate ${light ? "coord-dark" : "coord-light"}`}>{rankIndex + 1}</span>}
                      {session?.coordinates && visibleRow === 7 && <span className={`file-coordinate ${light ? "coord-dark" : "coord-light"}`}>{"abcdefgh"[fileIndex]}</span>}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
                <svg className="board-arrows" viewBox="0 0 8 8" aria-hidden="true">
                  <defs>
                    <marker id="user-arrowhead" markerWidth="0.28" markerHeight="0.28" refX="0.22" refY="0.14" orient="auto" markerUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
                      <path d="M0 0 L0.28 0.14 L0 0.28 Z" fill="#3277a8" />
                    </marker>
                    <marker id="engine-arrowhead" markerWidth="0.3" markerHeight="0.3" refX="0.24" refY="0.15" orient="auto" markerUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
                      <path d="M0 0 L0.3 0.15 L0 0.3 Z" fill="#d49b38" />
                    </marker>
                  </defs>
                  {[
                    ...userArrows.map((arrow) => ({ arrow, kind: "user" as const })),
                    ...(engineArrow ? [{ arrow: engineArrow, kind: "engine" as const }] : []),
                    ...(arrowStart && arrowHover && arrowStart !== arrowHover
                      ? [{ arrow: { from: arrowStart, to: arrowHover }, kind: "preview" as const }]
                      : []),
                  ].map(({ arrow, kind }, index) => {
                    const from = squareCenter(arrow.from, Boolean(session?.flipped));
                    const to = squareCenter(arrow.to, Boolean(session?.flipped));
                    const color = kind === "engine" ? "#d49b38" : kind === "preview" ? "#3277a8aa" : "#3277a8";
                    return <line
                      key={`${kind}-${arrow.from}-${arrow.to}-${index}`}
                      x1={from.x}
                      y1={from.y}
                      x2={to.x}
                      y2={to.y}
                      stroke={color}
                      strokeWidth={kind === "engine" ? 0.13 : 0.11}
                      strokeLinecap="round"
                      markerEnd={kind === "engine" ? "url(#engine-arrowhead)" : "url(#user-arrowhead)"}
                      className={`board-arrow board-arrow-${kind}`}
                    />;
                  })}
                </svg>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="board-controls">
            <button title="First move" onClick={() => jumpToMove(0)}>?</button>
            <button title="Previous move" onClick={() => jumpToMove(Math.max(0, (session?.moveIndex ?? 0) - 1))}>?</button>
            <button title="Next move" onClick={() => jumpToMove(Math.min(session?.moves.length ?? 0, (session?.moveIndex ?? 0) + 1))}>?</button>
            <button title="Last move" onClick={() => jumpToMove(session?.moves.length ?? 0)}>?</button>
            <span className="controls-separator" />
            <button title="Undo move" onClick={() => jumpToMove(Math.max(0, (session?.moveIndex ?? 0) - 1))}>?</button>
            <button title="Redo move" onClick={() => jumpToMove(Math.min(session?.moves.length ?? 0, (session?.moveIndex ?? 0) + 1))}>?</button>
            <button title="Reset to original diagram position" onClick={resetBoard}>? Reset</button>
            <button title="Toggle coordinates" onClick={() => patchSession({ coordinates: !session?.coordinates })}>Ã¢Å’â€”</button>
            <button title="Expand board" onClick={() => void expandBoard()}>Ã¢â€ºÂ¶</button>
            <button
              className={annotationMode ? "annotation-active" : ""}
              title="Touch alternative: tap two squares to add or remove an analysis arrow. Right-drag works with a mouse."
              aria-pressed={annotationMode}
              onClick={() => { setAnnotationMode((mode) => !mode); setArrowStart(null); setArrowHover(null); }}
            >Ã¢â€ â€” Arrows</button>
            {userArrows.length > 0 && <button title="Clear user analysis arrows" onClick={() => setUserArrows([])}>Clear arrows</button>}
            <span className="controls-spacer" />
            <button title="Copy current FEN" onClick={() => void copyText(game.fen())}>FEN</button>
            <button title="Copy current PGN" onClick={() => void copyText(game.pgn())}>PGN</button>
          </div>
          <div className="turn-row">
            <span>TO MOVE</span>
            <button className={whiteTurn ? "turn-active" : ""} onClick={() => {
              try {
                const fen = fenWithTurn(game.fen(), "w");
                patchSession({ lineBaseFen: fen, currentFen: fen, moves: [], moveIndex: 0 });
                setLastMove(null);
              } catch (cause) { setError(messageOf(cause)); }
            }}>Ã¢â€”â€¹ White</button>
            <button className={!whiteTurn ? "turn-active" : ""} onClick={() => {
              try {
                const fen = fenWithTurn(game.fen(), "b");
                patchSession({ lineBaseFen: fen, currentFen: fen, moves: [], moveIndex: 0 });
                setLastMove(null);
              } catch (cause) { setError(messageOf(cause)); }
            }}>Ã¢â€”Â Black</button>
            <button className="save-position" onClick={savePosition}>Ã¯Â¼â€¹ Save position</button>
          </div>
          <details className="position-editor">
            <summary>Board position Ã‚Â· enter FEN to correct selected diagram</summary>
            <div className="fen-entry"><input value={fenInput} onChange={(event) => setFenInput(event.target.value)} placeholder="Paste verified FEN from the selected diagram" aria-label="Position FEN input" /><button className="green-button" onClick={() => { if (!fenInput.trim()) { setError("Enter a FEN position first."); return; } if (moveToPosition(fenInput.trim())) setFenInput(""); }}>Load FEN</button></div>
          </details>
          <div className="moves-panel">
            <div className="moves-heading"><span>MOVE LIST</span><button className="new-line" onClick={() => {
              if (!session) return;
              patchSession({ lineBaseFen: game.fen(), currentFen: game.fen(), moves: [], moveIndex: 0 });
              setLastMove(null);
              setNotice("New analysis line started from the current position.");
            }}>Ã¯Â¼â€¹ New line</button></div>
            <div className="move-list">
              {moveHistory.length ? Array.from({ length: Math.ceil(moveHistory.length / 2) }, (_, row) => (
                <div className="move-pair" key={row}>
                  <span className="move-number">{row + 1}.</span>
                  {moveHistory[row * 2] && <button className={session?.moveIndex === row * 2 + 1 ? "active-move" : ""} onClick={() => jumpToMove(row * 2 + 1)}>{moveHistory[row * 2].san}</button>}
                  {moveHistory[row * 2 + 1] && <button className={session?.moveIndex === row * 2 + 2 ? "active-move" : ""} onClick={() => jumpToMove(row * 2 + 2)}>{moveHistory[row * 2 + 1].san}</button>}
                </div>
              )) : <span className="muted empty-moves">Select a position and play a move to start a line.</span>}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="board-fen"><span>{game.fen()}</span><span>{session?.moveIndex ? `MOVE ${session.moveIndex}` : "BOOK POSITION"}</span></div>
        </section>

        <section className="reader-panel" aria-label="PDF book reader">
          <div className="reader-toolbar">
            <div className="reader-title"><div className="document-icon">Ã¢â€“Â¤</div><div><strong>{book?.title ?? "Book reader"}</strong><span>{pdf ? `${session?.pageCount} pages Ã‚Â· PDF document` : "Add a PDF book to begin"}</span></div></div>
            <div className="reader-actions">
              <button className={session?.bookmarks.includes(session.page) ? "active-action" : ""} title="Bookmark this page" disabled={!book} onClick={saveBookmark}>Ã¢Å¡â€˜</button>
              <button title="Zoom out" disabled={!session} onClick={() => patchSession({ zoom: Math.max(0.65, (session?.zoom ?? 1) - 0.1) })}>Ã¢Ë†â€™</button>
              <span className="zoom-value">{Math.round((session?.zoom ?? 1) * 100)}%</span>
              <button title="Zoom in" disabled={!session} onClick={() => patchSession({ zoom: Math.min(1.8, (session?.zoom ?? 1) + 0.1) })}>Ã¯Â¼â€¹</button>
              <button title="Fit to width" disabled={!pdf} onClick={() => void fitPage("width")}>Fit width</button>
              <button title="Fit page" disabled={!pdf} onClick={() => void fitPage("page")}>Fit page</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="page-navigation">
            <button aria-label="Previous page" disabled={!pdf || session?.page === 1} onClick={() => changePage(-1)}>Ã¢â‚¬Â¹</button>
            <label>PAGE <input aria-label="Current page" type="number" min="1" max={session?.pageCount || 1} value={session?.page ?? 1} disabled={!pdf} onChange={(event) => goToPage(Number(event.target.value))} /> <span>of {session?.pageCount || "Ã¢â‚¬â€"}</span></label>
            <button aria-label="Next page" disabled={!pdf || (session?.page ?? 1) >= (session?.pageCount ?? 1)} onClick={() => changePage(1)}>Ã¢â‚¬Âº</button>
            <span className="nav-separator" />
            <button className="scan-button" disabled={!pdf || detecting || rendering} onClick={() => void detectBoards()}>{detecting ? <><span className="spinner" /> Detecting</> : "Ã¢Å’â€¢ Detect boards"}</button>
            <button className={`manual-button ${manualMode ? "manual-active" : ""}`} disabled={!pdf || rendering} title="Drag on the page to select a diagram region" onClick={() => setManualMode((active) => !active)}>{manualMode ? "Ãƒâ€” Cancel selection" : "Ã¯Â¼â€¹ Manual Detect Board"}</button>
            <span className="detection-status">{detecting ? "Scanning current pageÃ¢â‚¬Â¦" : currentCandidates.length ? `${currentCandidates.length} candidate${currentCandidates.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}` : detectionAttempted ? "No boards found" : "Not scanned"}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="page-workspace">
            {!book && <div className="reader-empty"><div className="empty-glyph">Ã¢â€“Â¤</div><strong>Open a chess book</strong><span>Import a PDF to read pages and mark chess diagrams.</span><button className="green-button" onClick={() => fileInput.current?.click()}>Ã¯Â¼â€¹ Add a book</button></div>}
            {book && (
              <div className="page-scroll">
                <div
                  className={`page-stage ${rendering ? "is-rendering" : ""} ${manualMode ? "manual-mode" : ""}`}
                  ref={pageStageRef}
                  onPointerDown={startRegion}
                  onPointerMove={moveRegion}
                  onPointerUp={finishRegion}
                  onPointerCancel={cancelRegion}
                >
                  <canvas ref={canvasRef} aria-label={`PDF page ${session?.page ?? 1}`} />
                  {!pdf && !pdfLoading && <div className="page-error">PDF is unavailable. Your saved reading state has been kept.</div>}
                  {currentCandidates.map((candidate, index) => (
                    candidate.id === cropEditingId ? (
                      <div
                        key={candidate.id}
                        className="diagram-overlay diagram-selected crop-editor"
                        style={{ left: `${candidate.x * 100}%`, top: `${candidate.y * 100}%`, width: `${candidate.width * 100}%`, height: `${candidate.height * 100}%` }}
                        aria-label={`Adjust crop region ${index + 1}`}
                        onPointerDown={(event) => startCropEdit(event, candidate, "move")}
                      >
                        <span>{index + 1} Ã‚Â· Drag to move</span>
                        {CROP_HANDLES.map((handle) => <button
                          key={handle}
                          type="button"
                          className={`crop-handle handle-${handle}`}
                          aria-label={`Resize crop ${handle}`}
                          onPointerDown={(event) => startCropEdit(event, candidate, handle)}
                        />)}
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <button
                        key={candidate.id}
                        className={`diagram-overlay ${candidate.id === session?.selectedDiagramId ? "diagram-selected" : ""} ${candidate.source === "manual" ? "manual-overlay" : ""}`}
                        style={{ left: `${candidate.x * 100}%`, top: `${candidate.y * 100}%`, width: `${candidate.width * 100}%`, height: `${candidate.height * 100}%` }}
                        aria-label={`Select diagram region ${index + 1}`}
                        onPointerDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
                        onClick={(event) => { event.stopPropagation(); selectCandidate(candidate); if (candidate.source === "automatic") void openTransfer(candidate); }}
                      ><span>{index + 1} Ã‚Â· {candidate.source === "manual" ? "Manual crop" : "Relative match"}</span></button>
                    )
                  ))}
                  {dragSelection && <div className="selection-rectangle" style={{ left: `${Math.min(dragSelection.x, dragSelection.x2) * 100}%`, top: `${Math.min(dragSelection.y, dragSelection.y2) * 100}%`, width: `${Math.abs(dragSelection.x2 - dragSelection.x) * 100}%`, height: `${Math.abs(dragSelection.y2 - dragSelection.y) * 100}%` }} />}
                  {rendering && <div className="rendering-label"><span className="spinner" /> Rendering page</div>}
                  {pdfLoading && <div className="rendering-label"><span className="spinner" /> Opening PDF</div>}
                </div>
                <div className="page-scroll-hint">Drag over the page to mark a diagram Ã‚Â· {currentCandidates.length} regions</div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="reader-footer">
            <div className="diagram-strip"><span className="strip-label">DIAGRAMS</span>{currentCandidates.length ? currentCandidates.map((candidate, index) => <button key={candidate.id} className={candidate.id === session?.selectedDiagramId ? "strip-selected" : ""} onClick={() => selectCandidate(candidate)} aria-label={`Select diagram ${index + 1}`}>{index + 1}</button>) : <span className="muted">None detected on this page</span>}</div>
            {currentDiagram && <div className="crop-actions">
              {cropEditingId === currentDiagram.id && <button onClick={resetSelectedCrop}>Reset crop</button>}
              <button onClick={() => adjustSelectedCrop(currentDiagram)}>{cropEditingId === currentDiagram.id ? "AdjustingÃ¢â‚¬Â¦" : "Adjust Crop"}</button>
              <button className="green-button" onClick={() => void openTransfer(currentDiagram)}>Recognize Pieces</button>
              <button onClick={cancelCandidateSelection}>Cancel</button>
            </div>}
            <div className="scan-hint">{currentDiagram ? "Selection does not change the board; confirm with Use on Board." : "Select a detected board or draw a region."}</div>
          </div>
          <div className="analysis-strip">
            <div className="analysis-name"><span className="stockfish-mark">Ã¢â„¢Å¾</span><div><strong>Stockfish 19 Lite</strong><span>{engineStatus === "error" ? "Engine unavailable" : engineStatus === "off" ? "Engine off" : engineStatus === "loading" ? "Loading engineÃ¢â‚¬Â¦" : engineStatus === "analyzing" ? "Analyzing current positionÃ¢â‚¬Â¦" : "Ready Ã‚Â· White-positive scores"}</span></div></div>
            <div className="analysis-divider" />
            <button className={`engine-toggle ${engineEnabled ? "engine-active" : ""}`} onClick={toggleEngine}>{engineEnabled ? "Stop analysis" : engineStatus === "error" ? "Retry Stockfish" : "Analyze position"}</button>
            <label className="depth-control">Depth <select value={engineDepth} onChange={(event) => setEngineDepth(Number(event.target.value))}><option value={10}>10</option><option value={12}>12</option><option value={14}>14</option><option value={16}>16</option><option value={18}>18</option></select></label>
            <div className="analysis-result"><span>Evaluation Ã‚Â· White</span><strong>{engineScore ? formatEvaluation(engineScore) : "Ã¢â‚¬â€"}</strong></div>
            <div className="analysis-result best-result"><span>Best move</span><strong>{engineScore?.bestMove ?? "Ã¢â‚¬â€"}</strong></div>
            <div className="analysis-pv"><span>PRINCIPAL VARIATION</span><strong>{engineStatus === "error" ? engineMessage : engineScore?.pv?.join(" ") ?? (engineEnabled ? "Waiting for first valid scoreÃ¢â‚¬Â¦" : "Enable Stockfish to analyze this position.")}</strong></div>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      {settingsOpen && <div className="settings-popover"><strong>Workspace settings</strong><span>Books and analysis sessions are stored in IndexedDB on this device.</span><span>Stockfish runs locally in a browser worker. Cloud sync is not configured.</span><button onClick={() => setSettingsOpen(false)}>Close</button></div>}
      {transferCandidate && <div className="modal-backdrop" role="presentation">
        <section className="transfer-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="transfer-title">
          <header><div><h2 id="transfer-title">Review recognized position</h2><p>The scan is already loaded on the analysis board. Review and correct any uncertain squares, then confirm your edits.</p></div><button aria-label="Close crop preview" onClick={closeTransfer}>Ãƒâ€”</button></header>
          <div className="recognition-layout">
            {transferPreview && <img className="crop-preview" src={transferPreview} alt="Exact selected chess diagram crop" />}
            <div className="recognition-summary" role="status">
              {recognitionStatus === "loading" && <><span className="spinner" /> Recognizing piecesÃ¢â‚¬Â¦</>}
              {recognitionStatus === "complete" && <>
                <strong>{recognitionEdited ? "Model result Ã‚Â· Manually corrected" : "Model-generated position"}</strong>
                <span>{recognitionResult?.piece_count ?? 0} pieces Ã‚Â· board score {recognitionResult?.board_confidence.toFixed(2)} Ã‚Â· piece scores are uncalibrated</span>
                <span>Orientation: {recognitionResult?.orientation ?? "unknown"}; confirm against the source crop.</span>
              </>}
              {recognitionStatus === "failed" && <><strong>Automatic recognition did not complete.</strong><span>{recognitionError}</span><span>Review the source crop and enter or correct its FEN manually.</span></>}
            </div>
          </div>
          {recognitionStatus === "complete" && transferBoard && <div className="recognized-position">
            <div className="recognized-board" role="group" aria-label="Editable recognized chess position">
              {transferBoard.flatMap((rank, row) => rank.map((piece, col) => {
                const light = (row + col) % 2 === 1;
                return <button
                  key={`${row}-${col}`}
                  type="button"
                  className={`recognized-square ${light ? "light-square" : "dark-square"}`}
                  aria-label={`Rank ${8 - row}, file ${"abcdefgh"[col]}: ${PIECE_LABELS[piece]}`}
                  title={`${"abcdefgh"[col]}${8 - row} Ã‚Â· ${PIECE_LABELS[piece]}`}
                  onClick={() => editTransferSquare(row, col)}
                >{piece && <svg viewBox="0 0 40 40" aria-hidden="true"><use href={`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}assets/pieces/standard.svg#${PIECE_ASSETS[piece]}`} /></svg>}</button>;
              }))}
            </div>
            <div className="recognition-edit-controls">
              <label>Piece to place
                <select value={transferPiece} onChange={(event) => setTransferPiece(event.target.value)}>
                  {Object.entries(PIECE_LABELS).map(([code, label]) => <option key={code || "empty"} value={code}>{label}</option>)}
                </select>
              </label>
              <button type="button" onClick={flipTransferPosition}>Flip position 180Ã‚Â°</button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => void openTransfer(transferCandidate)}>Retry recognition</button>
            </div>
          </div>}
          {recognitionResult?.warnings.map((warning) => <p className="recognition-warning" key={warning}>{warning}</p>)}
          {recognitionError && recognitionStatus !== "failed" && <p className="recognition-error" role="alert">{recognitionError}</p>}
          {recognitionStatus === "failed" && <div className="recognition-retry"><button type="button" onClick={() => void openTransfer(transferCandidate)}>Retry recognition</button></div>}
          <label className="transfer-fen">FEN Ã‚Â· edit manually if needed
            <input value={transferFen} onChange={(event) => { setTransferFen(event.target.value); setRecognitionEdited(true); setRecognitionError(""); }} placeholder="Paste a six-field FEN position" autoComplete="off" spellCheck={false} />
          </label>
          {transferFen && <p className={transferFenValid ? "fen-validation valid-fen" : "fen-validation"}>{transferFenValid ? "Syntactically valid chess position Ã‚Â· visually verify it against the diagram." : "FEN is not a valid chess position yet."}</p>}
          <footer><button onClick={closeTransfer}>Cancel</button><button className="green-button" disabled={!transferFenValid} onClick={confirmTransfer}>Use on Board</button></footer>
        </section>
      </div>}
      {(error || notice) && <div className={`toast ${error ? "toast-error" : ""}`} role={error ? "alert" : "status"}><span>{error || notice}</span><button aria-label="Dismiss message" onClick={() => { setError(""); setNotice(""); }}>Ãƒâ€”</button></div>}
    </main>
  );
}

function LibraryNavItem({ active, onClick, icon, label }: { active: boolean; onClick: () => void; icon: string; label: string }) {
  return <button className={`library-nav-item ${active ? "nav-active" : ""}`} onClick={onClick}><span>{icon}</span>{label}</button>;
}

function formatSize(bytes: number): string {
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${Math.max(1, Math.round(bytes / 1024))} KB`;
  return `${(bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB`;
}

function isTypingTarget(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  return target instanceof HTMLElement && (target.isContentEditable || ["INPUT", "TEXTAREA", "SELECT"].includes(target.tagName));
}

========== FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\CoordinateTraining.tsx ==========
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import "./coordinate-training.css";

type Orientation = "white" | "black";
type Mode = "timed" | "untimed" | "streak";
type SessionResult = { date: string; score: number; accuracy: number; duration: number };

const FILES = "abcdefgh";
const RANKS = "12345678";
const randomSquare = (avoid?: string) => {
  let next = `${FILES[Math.floor(Math.random() * 8)]}${RANKS[Math.floor(Math.random() * 8)]}`;
  while (next === avoid) next = `${FILES[Math.floor(Math.random() * 8)]}${RANKS[Math.floor(Math.random() * 8)]}`;
  return next;
};
const STORAGE_KEY = "chessbook-coordinate-training-v1";

export default function CoordinateTraining() {
  const [orientation, setOrientation] = useState<Orientation>("white");
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>("timed");
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(60);
  const [remaining, setRemaining] = useState(60);
  const [target, setTarget] = useState(() => randomSquare());
  const [score, setScore] = useState(0);
  const [correct, setCorrect] = useState(0);
  const [wrong, setWrong] = useState(0);
  const [streak, setStreak] = useState(0);
  const [bestStreak, setBestStreak] = useState(0);
  const [startedAt, setStartedAt] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<"correct" | "wrong" | "">("");
  const [sound, setSound] = useState(false);
  const [showCoordinates, setShowCoordinates] = useState(true);
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<SessionResult[]>(() => {
    try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) || "[]") as SessionResult[]; } catch { return []; }
  });
  const finished = mode === "timed" && remaining <= 0;
  const attempts = correct + wrong;
  const accuracy = attempts ? Math.round((correct / attempts) * 100) : 0;
  const squares = useMemo(() => {
    const files = orientation === "white" ? [...FILES] : [...FILES].reverse();
    const ranks = orientation === "white" ? [...RANKS].reverse() : [...RANKS];
    return ranks.flatMap((rank) => files.map((file) => `${file}${rank}`));
  }, [orientation]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!startedAt || finished || mode !== "timed") return;
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setRemaining((value) => Math.max(0, value - 1)), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [startedAt, finished, mode]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (finished && startedAt) {
      const result = { date: new Date().toISOString(), score, accuracy, duration: duration - remaining };
      setHistory((previous) => {
        const next = [result, ...previous].slice(0, 30);
        try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(next)); } catch { /* storage may be unavailable */ }
        return next;
      });
      setStartedAt(null);
    }
  }, [finished]);

  function restart(nextMode = mode, nextDuration = duration) {
    setMode(nextMode);
    setDuration(nextDuration);
    setRemaining(nextDuration);
    setTarget(randomSquare());
    setScore(0); setCorrect(0); setWrong(0); setStreak(0);
    setFeedback(""); setStartedAt(null);
  }

  function chooseSquare(square: string) {
    if (finished) return;
    if (!startedAt) setStartedAt(Date.now());
    if (square === target) {
      setCorrect((value) => value + 1);
      setScore((value) => value + 1);
      setStreak((value) => { const next = value + 1; setBestStreak((best) => Math.max(best, next)); return next; });
      setFeedback("correct");
    } else {
      setWrong((value) => value + 1);
      setStreak(0);
      setFeedback("wrong");
    }
    setTarget(randomSquare(target));
  }

  return <main className="coordinate-training-workspace">
    <header className="ct-header">
      <div><span className="ct-eyebrow">CHESSBOOK TRAINING</span><h1>Coordinate Training</h1><p>Build instant board vision. Find the square before the clock runs out.</p></div>
      <div className="ct-header-actions"><button onClick={() => restart()}>↻ Restart</button><button className="ct-primary" onClick={() => restart(mode, duration)}>New session</button></div>
    </header>
    <div className="ct-layout">
      <section className="ct-board-card">
        <div className="ct-board-top"><span>Find this square</span><strong className="ct-target">{finished ? "TIME!" : target}</strong><span className={`ct-feedback ${feedback}`}>{feedback === "correct" ? "Correct" : feedback === "wrong" ? "Try the next one" : "Select a square"}</span></div>
        <div className="ct-board" role="group" aria-label="Coordinate training chessboard">
          {squares.map((square) => {
            const file = FILES.indexOf(square[0]);
            const rank = Number(square[1]);
            const dark = (file + rank) % 2 === 0;
            return <button key={square} type="button" disabled={finished} onClick={() => chooseSquare(square)} className={`ct-square ${dark ? "dark" : "light"}`} aria-label={square}>
              {showCoordinates && ((orientation === "white" && file === 0) || (orientation === "black" && file === 7)) && <span className="ct-rank">{rank}</span>}
              {showCoordinates && ((orientation === "white" && rank === 1) || (orientation === "black" && rank === 8)) && <span className="ct-file">{square[0]}</span>}
            </button>;
          })}
        </div>
        <div className="ct-board-footer"><span>{orientation === "white" ? "White perspective" : "Black perspective"}</span><button onClick={() => setOrientation((value) => value === "white" ? "black" : "white")}>⇅ Flip board</button></div>
      </section>
      <aside className="ct-side">
        <section className="ct-card ct-stats"><div className="ct-card-heading">SESSION</div><div className="ct-timer">{mode === "untimed" ? "∞" : mode === "streak" ? "—" : `00:${String(remaining).padStart(2, "0")}`}</div><div className="ct-progress"><i style={{ width: `${mode === "timed" ? ((duration - remaining) / duration) * 100 : 0}%` }} /></div>
          <div className="ct-stat-grid"><div><strong>{score}</strong><span>Score</span></div><div><strong>{accuracy}%</strong><span>Accuracy</span></div><div><strong>{streak}</strong><span>Streak</span></div><div><strong>{bestStreak}</strong><span>Best streak</span></div></div>
          {!startedAt && <p className="ct-start-note">Your timer starts with your first answer.</p>}
          {finished && <div className="ct-finished">Session complete · {score} correct squares</div>}
        </section>
        <section className="ct-card"><div className="ct-card-heading">TRAINING SETTINGS</div><label>Mode<select value={mode} onChange={(e) => restart(e.target.value as Mode)}><option value="timed">Timed challenge</option><option value="untimed">Untimed practice</option><option value="streak">Streak challenge</option></select></label>
          <label>Duration<select value={duration} onChange={(e) => restart(mode, Number(e.target.value))}><option value={30}>30 seconds</option><option value={60}>60 seconds</option><option value={120}>2 minutes</option><option value={180}>3 minutes</option></select></label>
          <label className="ct-toggle"><input type="checkbox" checked={showCoordinates} onChange={(e) => setShowCoordinates(e.target.checked)} /> Show board coordinates</label>
          <label className="ct-toggle"><input type="checkbox" checked={sound} onChange={(e) => setSound(e.target.checked)} /> Sound feedback (coming soon)</label>
          <button className="ct-primary ct-full" onClick={() => restart()}>Start / Restart training</button>
        </section>
        <section className="ct-card"><div className="ct-card-heading">RECENT SESSIONS</div>{history.length ? history.slice(0, 4).map((item, index) => <div className="ct-history-row" key={`${item.date}-${index}`}><span>{new Date(item.date).toLocaleDateString()}</span><strong>{item.score} correct</strong><em>{item.accuracy}%</em></div>) : <p className="ct-empty">Your completed timed sessions will appear here.</p>}</section>
      </aside>
    </div>
  </main>;
}



========== FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\position-editor.test.ts.before_coordinate_fix.bak ==========
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { flipFenPlacement, parseFenPlacement, setFenPiece, withFenPlacement } from "./position-editor";

const START_PLACEMENT = "rnbqkbnr/pppppppp/8/8/8/8/PPPPPPPP/RNBQKBNR";

describe("position editor", () => {
  it("parses compressed ranks into all 64 cells", () => {
    const board = parseFenPlacement(START_PLACEMENT);
    expect(board).toHaveLength(8);
    expect(board?.[0]).toEqual(["r", "n", "b", "q", "k", "b", "n", "r"]);
    expect(board?.[3]).toEqual(Array(8).fill(""));
  });

  it("adds and removes pieces without changing other squares", () => {
    expect(setFenPiece("8/8/8/8/8/8/8/8", 4, 3, "Q")).toBe("8/8/8/3Q4/8/8/8/8");
    expect(setFenPiece("8/8/8/3Q4/8/8/8/8", 4, 3, "")).toBe("8/8/8/8/8/8/8/8");
  });

  it("flips all squares and retains the rest of a FEN", () => {
    expect(flipFenPlacement(START_PLACEMENT)).toBe(START_PLACEMENT);
    expect(withFenPlacement(`${START_PLACEMENT} b - - 0 1`, "8/8/8/8/8/8/8/8"))
      .toBe("8/8/8/8/8/8/8/8 b - - 0 1");
  });

  it("rejects malformed placements", () => {
    expect(parseFenPlacement("9/8/8/8/8/8/8/8")).toBeNull();
    expect(parseFenPlacement("8/8/8/8/8/8/8")).toBeNull();
  });
});
